Spectral sqrt for real P: Given Q complex array length N representing desired self-convolution (DFT). Need compute P such that P^2=Q and P is DFT of real sequence (P[N-k]=conj(P[k])). For each pair k and N-k:
Q0=Q[k], Q1=Q[N-k] ideally conj. We can average: Q_sym = 0.5*(Q[k]+conj(Q[N-k]))? Since Q should be real? Wait Q for real sequence a is DFT of real q; Q satisfies Q[N-k]=conj(Q[k]). We want P also real DFT. If we take principal sqrt of Q[k] and set P[N-k]=conj(sqrt(Q[k]]), then P^2[k]=Q[k], P^2[N-k]=conj(Q[k])=Q[N-k] if Q symmetric. Need first symmetrize Q: Qs[k]=0.5*(Q[k]+conj(Q[N-k]))? For k=0,N/2 ensure real. For a pair, if Qs[k] = (Q[N-k])^*, then sqrt pair consistent. What if Qs[k] negative real? Principal sqrt gives imaginary phase; okay P complex with conjugate pair -> real inverse. However P may not correspond to a nonnegative sequence; project.
But there is subtlety: Q = fft of q_target real -> Q symmetric. If we symmetrize and sqrt, P^2 = Qs? For pair, P[N-k]=conj(P[k]); P^2[N-k]=conj(P[k])^2=conj(Qs[k])=Qs[N-k]. Good. So P^2=Qs.
Implementation:
```
def spectral_sqrt(Q, N):
    P = np.empty(N, complex)
    # sym
    for k in range(N//2+1):
       if k==0 or (N%2==0 and k==N//2): val=0.5*(Q[k]+Q.conj()[k]? actually real part)
       else:
          # use Q[k] and conj(Q[-k])
          val = 0.5*(Q[k] + np.conj(Q[N-k]))
       # to be safe, val = complex(np.real(val), np.imag(val))
       P[k] = np.sqrt(val + 0j)
    for k in range(1, (N+1)//2): P[N-k] = np.conj(P[k])
    return P
```

```
def spectral_sqrt_from_Q(Q,N):
   idx = (-np.arange(N))%N
   Qs=0.5*(Q+np.conj(Q[idx]))
   P=np.sqrt(Qs+0j)
   # enforce
   if N%2==0:
      P[0]=np.sqrt(max(0.0, float(np.real(Qs[0])))) # or signed
      P[N//2]=np.sqrt(abs(float(np.real(Qs[N//2])))) # maybe sign
      for k in 1..N//2-1: P[N-k]=np.conj(P[k])
   else: ...
   return P
```

Algorithm details:

Helper `project_q_box(q, S2, M)`:
Input q nonnegative length L sum roughly S2. Return projected vector with max≤M sum S2. Need efficient.

Implementation:
```
def project_q_box(q, S2, M):
    q = np.asarray(q, float)
    # If M*len(q) < S2: enlarge M or just normalize? Ensure M >= S2/L. 
    if M * len(q) < S2:
       M = S2/len(q)*(1+1e-9)
    # If all q<=M and sum S2, return q
    if q.max() <= M + 1e-12 and abs(q.sum()-S2)<...:
        return q
    # lambda bounds: low = q.min() - S2/len(q) - 1? But we can set lo = min(q)-max(M, S2/L), hi=max(q)+M? Simpler:
    lo = 0.0 # lambda >=0? But formula may need negative to raise lows. Need include negative.
    # x(lam)=clip(q-lam,0,M). As lam -> -inf, x_i=M for q_i>M+? -> sum L*M. As lam -> inf, sum0. We need sum S2 < L*M.
    lo = -M  # sum maybe? Could be too high? If all q_i large? q-lam with lam=-M: values >= q+M, capped M => M for all => L*M. Good. hi = q.max()+1 maybe sum0.
    lo = -M
    hi = float(q.max()) + 1.0
    # Ensure sum(lo)>=S2 and sum(hi)<=S2. If not adjust.
```
If q has very small values and M small, lo=-M may not yield all M if some q_i-lam<0? With lam=-M, q_i+M ≥M since q_i≥0 -> capped M. So sum=LM. Good. hi=qmax+M+1 gives q_i - hi ≤0 -> sum0. Good.

# adjust sum small drift by scaling? x.sum may not exactly due rounding; scale non-? Scaling could exceed M. Better adjust few elements.

Spectral square root details with branch choices. Need choose signs to maximize nonneg inverse. The square root has sign ambiguity per pair: P_k can be ± principal sqrt, P_{N-k}=∓? Instead of principal sqrt of Q_t, choose among ±sqrt(Q_t,k) the one with maximum real correlation to current P_k. That is, `P[k] = sign(dot(real?))`. This reduces discontinuity. Actually Q_t symmetric not purely real; sqrt has two global for pair: P_k candidate r, P_{N-k}=conj(r). The conjugate pair alternative is -r, -conj(r). So for each positive frequency pair, choose sign ±1, not 4, because P real DFT constraint: P_{N-k}=conj(P[k]). If P[k]=r, alternative -r. For k=0,N/2 also sign ±. So we can choose each pair's sign independently to match current P: if Re(r * conj(P_cur[k])) >=0 choose r else -r. For zero/N/2 choose sign to match real part. This is crucial!    if fsize is None: fsize=1<<((2*n-1)-1).bit_length()? Actually next_pow2(2*n-1)

Algorithm `gs_spectral(a, n, S, M_init, M_final, time, rng, beta? alpha)`:
```
a = normalize(a,S)
init c,a score
best=a,score
M = min(score*(1+1e-6), maybe score*0.99?)
iteration:
  P=fft(a,N)
  q=fft_selfconv(a) # or ifft(P*P)
  q_t = project_q_box(q, S*S, M)
  Q=fft(q_t,N)
  P_new = spectral_sqrt_nearest(Q, P, N)
  p = real(ifft(P_new))[:n]
  p = project_simplex(p,S)  # nonneg sum
  # maybe blend candidates:
  for γ in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1]:
     cand = project_simplex((1-γ)*a + γ*p, S)
     s = obj(cand)
     if s < current_score* (1+1e-8) or s < best: accept? We want progress. Use if s < current_score + 1e-7: a=cand; current_score=s; break
     if s < best: best=cand,best_score=s
  if no accept: maybe still a=p if score not too worse? accept if s < current_score + 0.005*score and rng? but may hurt.
  # adjust M: if best improved set M = best*(1+...); else gradually lower: M = max(M_final, M*(0.999?))
```

`project_q_box(q, S2, M)` can be vectorized. Let's implement robust:
```
def project_q_box(q, S2, M):
    L = q.size
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    # ensure sum
    s = q.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.full(L, S2/L)
    if s > 0: q = q * (S2 / s)
    # M at least average
    M = max(float(M), S2 / L)
    if q.max() <= M + 1e-12:
        return q
    # binary lambda
    lo = -M
    hi = float(q.max()) + M + 1.0
    # In case sum(lo) < S2? Should be L*M >=S2.
    for _ in range(50):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       x = np.clip(q - mid, 0.0, M)
       sx = x.sum()
       if sx > S2:
           lo = mid
       else:
           hi = mid
    x = np.clip(q - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, M)
    # adjust residual by small scaling of non-capped? 
    sx=x.sum()
    if sx > 0:
        x *= (S2 / sx)
    # after scaling may slightly >M; clip and one more small
    x = np.minimum(x, M)
    sx=x.sum()
    # if still off, add to lowest values under M
    diff = S2 - sx
    if diff > 0:
        # choose elements with x < M-eps sorted? We can approximate add to below_M equally
        mask = x < M - 1e-12
        if mask.any():
            x[mask] = np.minimum(M, x[mask] + diff / mask.sum())
        else:
            x += diff / L
        x = np.minimum(x, M)
    elif diff < 0:
        mask = x > 0
        if mask.any():
            x[mask] = np.maximum(0, x[mask] + diff / mask.sum())
    return x
```
The scaling may violate M if x max was M and scale >1; but diff small. Better after binary sum almost S2; no need scale. We can skip scaling; instead adjust residual using mask. Use `diff` after clip; should be tiny. If binary not accurate enough, maybe 50 iterations enough double. `x.sum()` within 1e-9 relative. We can accept drift; Q sum not S2? FFT q sum drift may affect P DC; we can renormalize q_t sum before FFT: `x *= S2/x.sum()` but may exceed M by tiny; acceptable. Let's do renormalize, then if max >M, set M? Drift negligible. Use `x = x * (S2/sx); if x.max()>M: x[x>M]=M;` sum drift. Good enough.

# Set DC/Nyq to real nonnegative or matching current:

# P should be real for DFT real; if imag nonzero due Q, set real sign based on P_cur real
if N % 2 == 0:
    Ny = Qs[N//2].real

```

   P[0]=...; P[N//2]=...

else:

# If current very small, perhaps keep principal

Function `run_log_descent(a, n, S, budget, rng, beta_start=30, beta_end=500, use_top_frac=0.01)`:
Pseudo top-level:
```
def run_log_descent(a_in, n, S, budget_s, rng, beta_start=30.0, beta_end=800.0):
    a=normalize_seq(a_in,S)
    start=time.monotonic(); best=a; best_score=score_seq(a); current=best_score
    floor=S/(n*1e8)
    total_iters=0; no_improve=0
    # beta ramp over budget maybe 60% then active
    while time<dead:
       total_iters +=1
       t_norm=min(1.0,(time-start)/budget_s*0.8)
       beta=beta_start+(beta_end-beta_start)*t_norm
       c, A, N = fft_selfconv(a)
       current = c.max() # sum S? ensure a sum S
       if current < best_score - 1e-12: update
       # weights
       if rng.random() < 0.35:
          # top weights: choose top K with K maybe dynamic
          K = max(3, int(0.01*len(c)))
          # sample top? Use sort? Sorting length 16k every few okay, but frequent sort 1000*16k log= okay? Use argpartition for K.
          if K < len(c):
             idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
          else idx=np.arange(len(c))
          w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[idx]=1.0/K
       else:
          w=lse_weights(c,beta)
       # compute gradient
       W=fft(w,N)
       s=np.real(ifft(A * np.conj(W)))[:n]
       g=2.0 * a * np.maximum(s,0.0) # s should nonneg
       gm=g.max()
       if gm < 1e-30: # no gradient, perturb
          a = normalize_seq(a + rng.normal(0, 0.01*S/np.sqrt(n), n),S); continue
       # Try etas
       etas = np.array([0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]) # scale by gm? since normalized g/gm
       # Maybe randomize around 1
       best_eta_score=current; best_cand=a
       for eta in etas:
           # skip if eta too large causing all floor? 
           f = np.exp(-eta * (g / gm))
           cand = a * f
           cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
           cand = normalize_seq(cand,S)
           c_chk, _, _ = fft_selfconv(cand)
           sc = c_chk.max()
           if sc < best_eta_score:
              best_eta_score=sc; best_cand=cand
       if best_eta_score < current - 1e-12:
           a=np.maximum(best_cand,floor); current=best_eta_score
           if current<best_score: best=... 
           no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           # small perturbation
           if no_improve > 20:
               noise=0.02*S/np.sqrt(n)*np.exp(-no_improve/50.0)
               a=normalize_seq(a + rng.normal(0,noise,n),S)
               no_improve=0
       # optional: periodic spectral jump? handled outside.
    return best,best_score
```

But our update `a*=exp(-η 2a*s)` with normalization is gradient of F(y) on scale manifold ignoring normalization derivative. A more correct update for log score L(y)=log F(y)-2 logsumexp(y) would be gradient: g_i = 2 a_i s_i - 2 a_i (since derivative logsumexp wrt y_i = a_i/S, with S=1? More exactly after `a_new_i=a_i exp(-η g_i)`, sum = exp(-η∑ a_i g_i), normalization subtracts log sum: y_new_i = y_i -η g_i - log sum(new)/? Actually normalize by dividing by sum: y_new_i = y_i -η g_i - log(sum exp(y-ηg)). Since y normalized sum1, `log sum ≈ -η ∑ a_i g_i`. So y_new_i = y_i -η g_i + η ∑ a_i g_i = y_i -η (g_i - ∑a g). This is tangent projection with baseline mean weighted by a. Not the same as log-sumexp derivative 2a_i/S? Let's refine functions.

`normalize_seq(a, S)`:
```
def normalize_seq(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       return np.full_like(a, S/len(a))
    a = a * (S/s)
    # cap at 1000? If any >1000, clip and renormalize maybe.
    if a.max() > 1000.0:
       a = np.minimum(a,1000.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if s>0: a*=S/s
    return a
```
If clipping to 1000 at n small maybe sum impossible if S>1000n? n≥512, S~32, okay.

`fft_selfconv(a, N=None)`:
```
def fft_selfconv(a, N=None):
    n=len(a)
    if N is None:
       L=2*n-1
       N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=fft(a, N)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny negative
    # maybe symmetrize: c = 0.5*(c + c[::-1])? Self conv should symmetric; numerical. Do it.
    return c, A, N
```
Symmetrizing can reduce noise: `c = 0.5*(c + c[::-1]); c[:]=np.maximum(c,0)`. But if accurate, center? For linear self-convolution, symmetric yes. Do sym. Ensure c[0], c[-1] nonnegative.

`score_fft(a, N=None)`:
```
def score_fft(a,N=None):
   n=len(a); s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-300: return inf
   c,A,Nn=fft_selfconv(a,N)
   return 2*n*c.max()/(s*s)
```
If a normalized S, can just cmax. But use general for safety.

`lse_weights(c, beta)`:
```
def lse_weights(c, beta):
   cm=c.max()
   z=beta*(c-cm)
   # if beta large, z min may underflow okay
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: # fallback top
      idx=np.argmax(c); w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return
   return e/s
```
For beta=0 uniform. Ensure weights nonnegative sum1.

`top_weights(c, frac=0.01, rng=None)`:
```
def top_weights(c, frac=0.01, rng=None):
   L=len(c)
   K=max(1,int(frac*L))
   if K>=L: return np.full(L,1.0/L)
   if rng is not None and K < L//10? Could sample among top? For deterministic, use argpartition.
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0/K
   return w
```
argpartition each call may be costly but okay for occasional. Could use `np.sort`? Argpartition O(L). We'll use only 30% iterations.

`log_gradient_from_weights(a, W, N, n)` maybe:
```
def log_space_gradient(a, w, N):
   A=fft(a,N)
   W=fft(w,N)
   s=np.real(ifft(A * np.conj(W)))[:len(a)]
   s = np.maximum(s, 0.0)
   return 2.0 * a * s
```
But we already have A. To save, compute W and use A. Top-level functions accept A.

`multiplicative_update(a,g,eta,floor,S)`:
```
def multiplicative_update(a,g,eta,floor,S):
   gm=np.max(g)
   if gm < 1e-60: return a
   fac=np.exp(-eta*(g/gm))
   new=a*fac
   new=np.maximum(new,floor)
   return normalize_seq(new,S)
```
Could g have negative? clamp.

`run_log_descent` needs access to rng, budget. Let's top-level:
```
def run_log_descent(a_in, budget_s, rng, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=600.0, top_frac=0.015, use_top_prob=0.35, etas=(0.05,0.2,0.8,2.0)):
    n=len(a_in); S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_seq(a_in,S)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0,budget_s)
    best=a.copy(); current=score_fft(a); best_score=current
    floor=S/(n*1e8)
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    no_improve=0; iters=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       iters+=1
       c,A,N=fft_selfconv(a,N) # ensure N
       # If sum drift, normalize? a should.
       current = 2.0*n*c.max()/(a.sum()**2) # use actual
       if current < best_score - 1e-12: best=a.copy(); best_score=current
       tnorm = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/max(deadline-start,1.0))
       beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*min(1.0, tnorm*1.2)
       if rng.random() < use_top_prob:
           w = top_weights(c, top_frac)
       else:
           w = lse_weights(c, beta)
       W=fft(w,N)
       s=np.real(ifft(A * np.conj(W)))[:n]
       s=np.maximum(s,0.0)
       g=2.0*a*s
       # normalize g by objective scale? Use current + eps
       g /= (current + 1e-6)
       gm=g.max()
       if gm < 1e-30:
           a=normalize_seq(a + rng.normal(0,0.01*S/np.sqrt(n),n),S); no_improve=0; continue
       best_trial=current; best_cand=a
       # Adaptive eta around 1; use maybe previous? 
       for eta in etas:
           cand = multiplicative_update(a,g,eta,floor,S)
           sc,_ =? Need just score. We'll call c2,A2,_=fft_selfconv(cand,N); sc=2*n*c2.max()/(cand.sum()**2)
           if sc < best_trial:
              best_trial=sc; best_cand=cand
       if best_trial < current - 1e-11:
           a=best_cand; current=best_trial; no_improve=0
           if current < best_score - 1e-12: best=a.copy(); best_score=current
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           if no_improve > 15:
               # perturb or spectral? We can just noise
               noise=0.02*S/np.sqrt(n)*np.exp(-0.05*no_improve)
               a=normalize_seq(a + rng.normal(0,noise,n),S)
               no_improve=0
    return best,best_score
```

Function `run_spectral_gs(a_in, budget_s, rng, m_factor=0.995, m_min_factor=1.001)`:
```
def run_spectral_gs(a_in, budget_s, rng, m_relax=0.005):
   n=len(a_in); S=sqrt(2n); a=normalize(a,S); N=next_pow2(2n-1); start; best; current=score_fft(a)
   L=2n-1; avg=S2/L
   M=min(current*(1.0-m_relax), max(avg*(1+1e-6), current*0.98))
   stall=0; iters=0
   while time<deadline-2:
      c,A,N=fft_selfconv(a,N); current=2n*c.max()/S2
      if current<best... 
      # q = c, ensure nonneg sum S2
      q=np.maximum(c,0); qsum=q.sum(); if qsum>0: q*=S2/qsum
      M = max(avg*(1+1e-6), min(M, current*0.995)) # lower target if current high? 
      # if M > current maybe set current*0.995
      if M >= current: M = max(avg, current*0.995)
      q_t=project_q_box(q,S2,M)
      Q=fft(q_t,N)
      Pn=spectral_sqrt_nearest(Q,A,N)
      p=np.real(ifft(Pn))[:n]
      p=project_simplex(p,S)
      # candidates alpha
      best_trial=current; best_cand=a
      for alpha in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
         cand=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*p; cand=normalize_seq(cand,S)
         sc=_score_normalized(cand,N)
         if sc<best_trial: ...
      if best_trial < current - 1e-11:
          a=best_cand; current=best_trial; stall=0
          # tighten M
          M = max(avg, min(current*0.998, M*0.98))
      else:
          stall+=1
          if stall > 20:
             # relax M
             M = min(current*1.02, M*1.05)
             stall=0
      # gradually lower M
      M = max(avg, M * 0.9995)
   return best,best_score
```

Top-level helper `make_initial_starts(base, n, rng)`:
```
def make_initial_starts(base, n, rng):
    S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    starts=[]
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    # base resampled
    if base is not None: starts.append(("base", resample_seq(base,n)))
    # uniform
    starts.append(("uniform", np.ones(n)*S/n))
    # cosine edge
    starts.append(("cos_edge", 0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*t)))
    # center
    starts.append(("cos_center", 0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*(t-0.5))))
    # beta shapes alpha list
    for alpha in [0.25,1,2,4,10]:
       vec=t**alpha*(1-t)**alpha + 1e-6
       starts.append((("beta_%s")%alpha, vec))
    # random low frequency
    for rep in range(8):
       phase = rng.random(4) ...
       vec=1 + 0.4*cos(2π(t*1+φ)) +0.3*cos(4π(...)) ... clip
       starts.append
    # random nonnegative
    starts.append(("rand", rng.random(n)+0.1))
    # all normalize in normalize function
    return [(name, normalize_seq(arr,S)) ...]
```

`resample_seq(a,m)`:
```
def resample_seq(a,m):
   if len(a)==m: return a.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,len(a)-1,len(a))
   x_new=np.linspace(0,m-1,m)
   y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   # if upsampling, apply small Gaussian smoothing in frequency maybe
   if m>len(a):
      N=next_pow2(m); F=fft(y,N); freqs=np.arange(N); low=0.5*m/len(a)? Maybe zero high? But linear interp already.
   return np.maximum(y,0)
```

if s < S2:

   # add to elements below M equally
   mask = x < M

   # if still deficit, add to capped equally
   while ... (few)
elif s > S2:
   excess = s-S2
   # reduce elements above? But x max M, sum cannot exceed S2 if M feasible? If q max≤M, x=q sum S2. If M infeasible? 
return x
```

Algorithm:
```
def project_q_box(q,S2,M):
   L=q.size; M=max(M,S2/L*(1+1e-12))
   x=np.minimum(np.maximum(q,0), M)
   s=x.sum()
   if s <= S2:
      deficit=S2-s
      # if deficit ==0 return
      if deficit >0:
         # first add to values below M (all if none capped)
         # To minimize peaks, add to smallest values, but approximate: add equally to all values below M until maybe capped
         # We can do waterfill: if deficit <= (M - x.min())*count_below? one step maybe.
         # Simpler: add equally to all values < M; if some hit M, repeat few.
         for _ in range(5):
            mask = x < M - 1e-15
            cnt = mask.sum()
            if cnt == 0: break
            add = deficit / cnt
            if add <= M - x[mask].max() + 1e-15:
                x[mask] += add; deficit=0; break
            else:
                # raise all mask to M
                x[mask] = M
                deficit -= (M - x[mask_old?])? Need compute before.
        if deficit > 1e-9: x += deficit/L
   else:
      # s > S2 can happen if M<avg? but M>=avg; if q has many ≤M? x sum could be >S2? Since x≤q only, sum≤S2 because q sum S2; so not.
   # ensure sum exactly: if sdrift, distribute to below M
   s=x.sum(); diff=S2-s
   if diff>0: x+=diff/L; x=np.minimum(x,M)
```
This quickly caps high and raises all below evenly. It may not be exact L2 projection but okay. It preserves max ≤M. We can use this for speed. For exact, add one binary search if M very low? But simple enough.

Let's implement robust waterfill approximate:
```
def project_q_box(q,S2,M):
   L=q.size
   M=max(M, S2/L*(1+1e-12))
   x=np.clip(q,0.0,M)
   s=x.sum()
   diff=S2 - s
   if diff > 0:
      # add to lowest, but equally to all is okay; if M constraint:
      # Try up to 8 rounds: add to elements with x < M
      for _ in range(8):
          if diff <= 1e-12: break
          mask = x < M - 1e-15
          cnt = int(mask.sum())
          if cnt == 0:
              x += diff/L; break
          room = M - x[mask]
          maxroom = np.max(room)
          add = min(diff/cnt, maxroom)
          if add > 0:
              x[mask] += add
              diff -= add*cnt
          # if add limited by smallest? Actually if add = maxroom, some hit M, remove them. repeat.
      if diff > 1e-12:
          # add to all, then cap
          x += diff/L
          np.minimum(x,M,out=x)
   # if sum slightly off final renorm under M? Use small correction
   s=x.sum(); diff=S2-s
   if abs(diff) > 1e-10:
       if diff >0:
          mask=x < M-1e-12
          if mask.any(): x[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
          else: x+=diff/L
       else:
          mask=x > 1e-12
          if mask.any(): x[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
       np.clip(x,0,M,out=x)
   return x
```
This O(L*rounds) worst, but usually diff small after cap. Good.

If M just below current max, diff small, one round. Very fast.

` spectral_sqrt_nearest(Q, P_cur, N, rng, flip_prob)`:
```
def spectral_sqrt_nearest(Q, P_cur, N, rng=None, flip_prob=0.0):
   idx=(-np.arange(N))%N
   Qs=0.5*(Q+np.conj(Q[idx]))
   # make DC Nyq real nonneg
   if N%2==0:
      Qs[0]=max(0.0, float(np.real(Qs[0])))
      Qs[N//2]=max(0.0, float(np.real(Qs[N//2])))
   else:
      Qs[0]=max(0.0,float(np.real(Qs[0])))
   P=np.sqrt(Qs + 0j)
   if N%2==0:
      half=N//2
      P[0]=np.sqrt(Qs[0])
      P[half]=np.sqrt(Qs[half]) # real
      ks=np.arange(1,half)
   else:
      half=N//2
      P[0]=np.sqrt(Qs[0])
      ks=np.arange(1,half+1)
   if ks.size:
      cand=P[ks]
      if P_cur is not None:
         cur=P_cur[ks]
         dots=np.real(cand*np.conj(cur))
         signs=np.where(dots >=0, 1.0, -1.0)
         # if cur small, keep principal
         signs=np.where(np.abs(cur) < 1e-10, 1.0, signs)
      else:
         signs=np.ones(ks.size)
      if rng is not None and flip_prob >0:
         flip = rng.random(ks.size) < flip_prob
         signs = np.where(flip, -signs, signs)
      P[ks] = signs * cand
      P[N-ks] = np.conj(P[ks])
   return P
```
Note `P[N-ks]` where ks array; if N-ks includes 0? ks≥1, fine. For odd with ks includes half, N-half > half, fine.
If Qs[0] small, sqrt. P_cur[0] sign positive; okay.
But if Qs[N/2] negative, set to 0. This may change sum? DC unaffected. Good.

Potential numerical: `Qs=0.5*(Q+conj(Q[idx]))` for all N, then we set half; `P` for second half overwritten. Good.

Pseudo:
```
def run_spectral_gs(a_in, budget_s, rng, m_spacing=0.005):
   n=len(a_in); S=np.sqrt(2n); a=normalize_seq(a_in,S); S2=2n; N=next_pow2(2n-1)
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s
   current=normalized_score(a,N); best=a.copy(); best_score=current
   avg=S2/(2*n-1)
   M=min(current*(1.0-m_spacing), max(avg*(1+1e-8), current*0.98))
   stall=0; iters=0
   while time<deadline-2:
      iters+=1
      c,A,N=fft_selfconv(a,N)
      current=2*n*c.max()/(a.sum()**2) # a sum S
      if current<best_score... 
      q=np.maximum(c,0); qs=q.sum(); if qs>0: q*=S2/qs
      # M update
      if M >= current: M = max(avg, current*(1.0-m_spacing))
      # gradually tighten
      M = max(avg, min(M, current*0.998))
      q_t=project_q_box(q,S2,M)
      Q=fft(q_t,N)
      best_trial=current; best_cand=a
      # branches
      branch_probs=(0.0,0.08,0.25)
      for fp in branch_probs:
         if time > deadline-3: break
         Pn=spectral_sqrt_nearest(Q,A,N,rng,fp)
         p=np.real(ifft(Pn))[:n]
         p=project_simplex(p,S)
         # evaluate p and blends
         for alpha in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
             cand=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*p
             cand=normalize_seq(cand,S)
             sc,_,_=? normalized_score(cand,N)
             if sc<best_trial: ...
      if best_trial < current - 1e-11:
         a=best_cand; current=best_trial; stall=0
         if current<best: update
      else:
         stall+=1
         if stall>25:
            M = min(current*1.03, M*1.1)
            stall=0
   return best,best_score
```
But `normalized_score` does FFT. Branches 3*3=9 scores per iter. If GS 1000 iters ->9000 FFTs, maybe okay for N=16k but could be seconds? 9000*0.2ms=1.8s plus loop, fine. Actually FFT 16k in Python maybe 0.5ms ->4.5s. Good. For N=32768 maybe 1ms ->9s. okay. Could reduce branches to 2 and alphas 2 if time. `project_simplex` for p: Let's implement:
```
def project_simplex(v,S):
   n=len(v); v=np.asarray(v,float); w=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cum=np.cumsum(w); idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float); tc=(cum-S)/idx; valid=tc <= w; if any rho=max valid+1 else n; theta=(cum[rho-1]-S)/rho; u=np.maximum(v-theta,0); s=u.sum(); if s<... uniform; else u*=S/s; return u
```
This ensures sum S exactly and nonneg. Good. For p from ifft, might have large negative; OK.

`resample_seq`: Let's implement using FFT interpolation for smoother upsample:
```
def resample_seq(a,m):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a)
    if n==m: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,n-1.0,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,m-1.0,m)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,np.maximum(a,0.0))
    if m > n:
        # low-pass in frequency to reduce interpolation ripples
        N=1<<((m-1).bit_length())
        F=fft(y,N)
        max_f = N * (0.5 * n / m) # Nyquist of original approx
        freqs=np.abs(np.fft.fftfreq(N))*N
        F *= np.exp(-(freqs/max(1.0,max_f))**2)
        y=np.real(ifft(F))
    return np.maximum(y,0.0)
```
This smooths. If m<n, linear average perhaps; but we only upsample.

For low-res to main upsample, use this.

Detailed function implementations:

1. `next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m < 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()
```
Check: m=1 -> (0).bit_length()=0 ->1. m=2 -> (1).bit_length()=1 ->2. m=3 -> (2).bit_length()=2 ->4. Good.

2. `normalize_seq`:
```
def normalize_seq(a, S):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size==0:
       a=np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       return np.full(a.size, S/a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (S/s)
    if a.size > 0 and a.max() > 1000.0:
        a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
        s=a.sum()
        if s<1e-300: return np.full(a.size,S/a.size)
        a *= S/s
    return a
```
If S/a.size >1000? n at least 1, S<=181, okay.

3. `fft_selfconv`:
```
def fft_selfconv(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=next_pow2(L)
    if N < L: N=next_pow2(L)
    A=fft(a,N)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
    c=c[:L]
    # sym/clip small negatives
    c=0.5*(c + c[::-1])
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c,A,N
```
Sym c can change c[0] with c[-1] average; both small. Good. However if a not symmetric, self-convolution linear is symmetric: c_k = c_{L-1-k}. Sym good. But due FFT numeric, c may have tiny negative edges; max0. Sum maybe not S2; but we normalize q separately.

4. `score_fft`:
```
def score_fft(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    s=a.sum()
    if n==0 or s<1e-300: return np.inf
    c,_,Nn=fft_selfconv(a,N)
    return float(2.0*n*c.max()/(s*s))
```
For speed, we often know sum; okay.

5. `lse_weights`:
```
def lse_weights(c, beta):
    cm=float(c.max())
    if cm <= 0: return np.full(len(c),1/len(c))
    if beta <= 0: return np.full(len(c),1/len(c))
    z=beta*(c-cm)
    # clip underflow no issue
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[idx]=1; return w
    return e/s
```
For beta large (1000), beta*(c-cm) min -1000*1e-3 -1, okay. If diff -1, underflow to0.

6. `top_weights`:
```
def top_weights(c, frac=0.01, min_count=3):
    L=len(c); K=max(min_count,int(frac*L),1)
    if K>=L: return np.full(L,1.0/L)
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    w=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64); w[idx]=1.0/K
    return w
```
Argpartition if K==L? handled. If K close L, -K? For K=L-? okay. If K > L? no. For L small, K>=L.

7. `project_simplex`: as above.
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return np.ones(1)/1*S? 
    if n==1: return np.array([S])
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum=np.cumsum(w)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    tc=(cum-S)/idx
    valid=tc <= w
    if np.any(valid):
       rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    else:
       rho=n
    theta=(cum[rho-1]-S)/rho
    u=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=u.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       return np.full(n,S/n)
    u *= S/s
    return u
```
Need if valid none? For S positive and v maybe all -inf? It will. If `cum` and S large, okay. If rho maybe 0? tc <= w may none; rho=n. If S negative? no.

8. `project_q_box` simple water fill:
```
def project_q_box(q, S2, M):
    L=q.size
    M=float(M)
    if L==0: return q
    minM=S2/L*(1.0+1e-12)
    if M < minM: M=minM
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(q,dtype=np.float64),0.0,M)
    s=x.sum()
    diff=S2 - s
    if diff > 0:
        for _ in range(12):
            if diff <= 1e-12: break
            mask=x < M - 1e-15
            cnt=int(mask.sum())
            if cnt == 0:
                x = x + diff/L
                if x.max() > M: x = np.clip(x,0,M)
                break
            room=M - x[mask]
            max_room=float(room.max()) if room.size else 0.0
            add=diff/cnt
            if add > max_room:
                add=max_room
            if add <= 0:
                break
            x[mask] += add
            diff -= add*cnt
        if diff > 1e-12:
            x = x + diff/L
            np.minimum(x,M,out=x)
    # final correction
    s=x.sum()
    diff=S2 - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-10:
        if diff > 0:
            mask=x < M - 1e-12
            cnt=int(mask.sum())
            if cnt > 0:
                x[mask] += diff/cnt
            else:
                x += diff/L
            np.minimum(x,M,out=x)
        else:
            mask=x > 1e-12
            cnt=int(mask.sum())
            if cnt > 0:
                x[mask] += diff/cnt
            else:
                x += diff/L
            np.clip(x,0,M,out=x)
    return x
```
This may not exactly sum after final clip; maybe tiny. Good.

9. `spectral_sqrt_nearest` as above. Need vectorized signs and assignment.
```
def spectral_sqrt_nearest(Q, P_cur, N, rng=None, flip_prob=0.0):
    idx=(-np.arange(N)) % N
    Qs=0.5*(Q + np.conj(Q[idx]))
    # Force DC/Nyquist real nonnegative
    if N % 2 == 0:
        half=N//2
        dc=float(np.real(Qs[0])); if dc<0: dc=0.0
        ny=float(np.real(Qs[half])); if ny<0: ny=0.0
        Qs[0]=dc; Qs[half]=ny
    else:
        dc=float(np.real(Qs[0])); if dc<0: dc=0.0
        Qs[0]=dc
    P=np.sqrt(Qs + 0j)
    if N % 2 == 0:
        half=N//2
        P[0]=np.sqrt(dc)* (1+0j)  # positive; could sign match if P_cur negative but sum positive
        P[half]=np.sqrt(ny)* (1+0j)
        ks=np.arange(1,half)
    else:
        half=N//2
        P[0]=np.sqrt(dc)
        ks=np.arange(1,half+1)
    if ks.size:
       cand=P[ks]
       if P_cur is None:
          signs=np.ones(ks.size)
       else:
          cur=P_cur[ks]
          dots=np.real(cand*np.conj(cur))
          signs=np.where(dots >= 0.0, 1.0, -1.0)
          signs=np.where(np.abs(cur) < 1e-12, 1.0, signs)
       if rng is not None and flip_prob > 0.0:
          flip = rng.random(ks.size) < flip_prob
          signs = np.where(flip, -signs, signs)
       P[ks] = signs.astype(np.complex128) * cand
       P[N-ks] = np.conj(P[ks])
    return P
```
Potential issue: For even N, `ny` variable defined if even; if odd no `ny`. Use if. For P[0], we used original Qs[0]. If set Qs[0]=dc, P sqrt all may have positive; P[0] sqrt positive. Good.

10. `resample_seq` as above. Need `np.fft.fftfreq`? We can use `np.fft.fftfreq` direct. Use `max_f = max(1.0, N * 0.5 * n / m)` for upsample. If n/m maybe. For downsample, linear good.
```
def resample_seq(a,m):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a)
   if n==m: return a.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,n-1.0,n)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,m-1.0,m)
   y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,np.maximum(a,0.0))
   if m > n:
      N=next_pow2(m)
      F=fft(y,N)
      freqs=np.abs(np.fft.fftfreq(N))*N
      fmax=0.5*N*n/float(m)
      if fmax < 1: fmax=1
      F *= np.exp(-(freqs/fmax)**2)
      y=np.real(ifft(F))
   return np.maximum(y,0.0)
```
For upsample factor 2, fmax=0.5N*0.5=N/4, low-pass smooths.

13. `make_starts`:
```
def make_starts(base,n,rng, count_rand=6):
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    starts=[]
    if base is not None:
       b=resample_seq(base,n)
       b=normalize_seq(b,S)
       # slight noise
       b=normalize_seq(b + rng.normal(0,0.005*S/np.sqrt(n),n),S)
       starts.append(("base",b))
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    starts.append(("uniform", np.ones(n)*S/n))
    starts.append(("cos_edge", 0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*t)))
    starts.append(("cos_center", 0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*(t-0.5))))
    for alpha in (0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       v=t**alpha * (1-t)**alpha + 1e-4
       starts.append(("beta" + str(alpha), v))
    # asymmetric Gaussian
    for center in (0.25,0.35,0.65,0.75):
       v=np.exp(-((t-center)/0.12)**2)
       starts.append(("gauss%s"%center, v))
    # random low freq
    for i in range(count_rand):
       v=np.ones(n)
       for fr in (1,2,3,5):
          amp=0.25/rng.standard_normal? Better random [-0.4,0.4]
          amp=rng.uniform(0.0,0.4)
          phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          v += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*fr*t + phase)
       v=np.maximum(v,0.01)
       starts.append(("randlow%d"%i, v))
    # random
    starts.append(("rand", rng.random(n)+0.1))
    for name,v in starts:
       starts.append? Need avoid appending during loop. We'll build list raw then normalize.
```
Need ensure names use `%`: "beta_%g". Alpha float; use str.

```

def multiplicative_update(a,g,eta,floor,S):

Let's write `fft_selfconv` with option:
```
def fft_selfconv(a,N=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   if N is None: N=next_pow2(L)
   if N < L: N=next_pow2(L)
   A=fft(a,N)
   c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
   c=c[:L]
   np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
   return c,A,N
```
If N from previous is <L? pass correct.

def score_fft(a,N=None):

    c,_,Nn=fft_selfconv(a,N)
    return 2*n*c.max()/(s*s)
```

`make_starts` top-level. Need not use strings? It returns list.
```
def make_starts(base, n, rng, count_low=4):
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*float(n))
    raw=[]
    if base is not None:
        b=resample_seq(base,n)
        raw.append(("base", b))
    if n>1:
       t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    else:
       t=np.array([0.0])
    raw.append(("uniform", np.ones(n)*S/float(n)))
    raw.append(("cos_edge", 0.5+0.5*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*t)))
    raw.append(("cos_center", 0.5+0.5*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*(t-0.5))))
    raw.append(("linear", np.linspace(0.01,1.0,n)))
    for aexp in (0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       v = t**aexp * (1.0-t)**aexp + 1e-4
       raw.append(("beta_%g"%aexp, v))
    for center in (0.2,0.3,0.7,0.8):
       v = np.exp(-(((t-center)/0.10)**2))
       raw.append(("gauss_%g"%center, v))
    for i in range(count_low):
       v=np.ones(n)
       for fr in (1,2,3,5):
          v = v + rng.uniform(0.0,0.35)*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*fr*t + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
       v=np.maximum(v,0.01)
       raw.append(("randlow_%d"%i, v))
    raw.append(("rand", rng.random(n)+0.1))
    res=[]
    noise_base=0.002*S/np.sqrt(max(1,n))
    for name,v in raw:
       ar=normalize_seq(v,S)
       ar=normalize_seq(ar + rng.normal(0.0,noise_base,n),S)
       res.append((name,ar))
    return res
```
Names with `%g` okay. For n=1, t array length1; cos okay. Gloss.

Potential issue: `linear` start normalized makes sum S; asymmetric. Good.

```python
"""
Improved search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(a*a) / (sum(a))^2.

Main ingredients:
- FFT-based quadratic objective evaluation.
- Natural-gradient / multiplicative updates in log-amplitude space.
  This exploits the log-convex structure of each convolution coefficient.
- Spectral square-root Gerchberg-Saxton style projections:
  enforce a flattened / box-projected convolution in the frequency domain
  by taking a square root of its DFT, with branch-sign search.
- Coarse-to-fine low-resolution restarts and limited length doubling.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import operator
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


def next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m (minimum 1)."""
    m = int(m)
    if m < 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_seq(a, S):
    """Clamp to non-negative finite floats and rescale to sum S."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([float(S)], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, float(S) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (float(S) / s)
    if a.size and float(a.max()) > 1000.0:
        a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 1e-300:
            return np.full(a.size, float(S) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
        a = a * (float(S) / s)
    return a


def fft_selfconv(a, N=None):
    """Linear self-convolution via zero-padded FFT. Returns (c, A, N)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None or int(N) < L:
        N = next_pow2(L)
    else:
        N = int(N)
    A = fft(a, N)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    c = c[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c, A, N


def score_fft(a, N=None):
    """Internal objective using FFT. For sum normalized to sqrt(2n), equals max c."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    c, _, _ = fft_selfconv(a, N)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()) / (s * s))


def lse_weights(c, beta):
    """Stable softmax weights for log-sum-exp smoothing of max(c)."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.ones(0)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if cm <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    z = beta * (c - cm)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def top_weights(c, frac=0.01, min_count=3):
    """Uniform weights on the largest fraction of convolution lags."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.ones(0)
    K = max(int(min_count), int(frac * L), 1)
    if K >= L:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / K
    return w


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum x = S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([float(S)], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(S)], dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    tc = (cum - float(S)) / idx
    valid = tc <= w
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    else:
        rho = n
    theta = (cum[rho - 1] - float(S)) / float(rho)
    u = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(u.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, float(S) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    u = u * (float(S) / s)
    return u


def project_q_box(q, S2, M):
    """
    Cheaply project a non-negative q with sum S2 into {0 <= q <= M, sum ~= S2}.
    This is not exact L2 projection but preserves max <= M and sum.
    """
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    L = q.size
    if L == 0:
        return q
    M = float(M)
    minM = float(S2) / float(L) * (1.0 + 1e-12)
    if M < minM:
        M = minM
    x = np.clip(q, 0.0, M)
    diff = float(S2) - float(x.sum())
    if diff > 0.0:
        for _ in range(16):
            if diff <= 1e-12:
                break
            mask = x < (M - 1e-15)
            cnt = int(mask.sum())
            if cnt == 0:
                x = x + diff / L
                np.minimum(x, M, out=x)
                break
            room = M - x[mask]
            min_room = float(room.min()) if room.size else 0.0
            add = min(diff / float(cnt), min_room)
            if add <= 0.0:
                break
            x[mask] += add
            diff -= add * float(cnt)
        if diff > 1e-12:
            x = x + diff / L
            np.minimum(x, M, out=x)
    # Small final correction.
    diff = float(S2) - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-10:
        if diff > 0:
            mask = x < (M - 1e-12)
            cnt = int(mask.sum())
            if cnt > 0:
                x[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
            else:
                x += diff / L
            np.minimum(x, M, out=x)
        else:
            mask = x > 1e-12
            cnt = int(mask.sum())
            if cnt > 0:
                x[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
            else:
                x += diff / L
            np.clip(x, 0.0, M, out=x)
    return x


def spectral_sqrt_nearest(Q, P_cur, N, rng=None, flip_prob=0.0):
    """
    Given Q = FFT(desired convolution), find P such that P^2 ~= Q and P is
    consistent with a real sequence.  Branch signs are chosen nearest to P_cur
    and randomly flipped with probability flip_prob.
    """
    idx = (-np.arange(N)) % N
    Qs = 0.5 * (Q + np.conj(Q[idx]))

    if N % 2 == 0:
        half = N // 2
        dc = float(np.real(Qs[0]))
        ny = float(np.real(Qs[half]))
        if dc < 0.0:
            dc = 0.0
        if ny < 0.0:
            ny = 0.0
        Qs[0] = dc
        Qs[half] = ny
    else:
        half = N // 2
        dc = float(np.real(Qs[0]))
        if dc < 0.0:
            dc = 0.0
        Qs[0] = dc

    P = np.sqrt(Qs + 0j)

    if N % 2 == 0:
        P[0] = np.sqrt(max(0.0, float(dc))) + 0j
        P[half] = np.sqrt(max(0.0, float(ny))) + 0j
        ks = np.arange(1, half)
    else:
        P[0] = np.sqrt(max(0.0, float(dc))) + 0j
        ks = np.arange(1, half + 1)

    if ks.size:
        cand = P[ks]
        if P_cur is None:
            signs = np.ones(ks.size, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            cur = P_cur[ks]
            dots = np.real(cand * np.conj(cur))
            signs = np.where(dots >= 0.0, 1.0, -1.0)
            signs = np.where(np.abs(cur) < 1e-12, 1.0, signs)
        if rng is not None and flip_prob > 0.0:
            flip = rng.random(ks.size) < float(flip_prob)
            signs = np.where(flip, -signs, signs)
        P[ks] = signs.astype(np.complex128) * cand
        P[N - ks] = np.conj(P[ks])
    return P


def resample_seq(a, m):
    """Resample a 1D sequence to length m, with gentle low-pass on upsampling."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(a)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, max(0, n - 1), n) if n > 1 else np.array([0.0])
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, max(0, m - 1), m)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.maximum(a, 0.0))
    if m > n:
        N = next_pow2(m)
        F = fft(y, N)
        freqs = np.abs(np.fft.fftfreq(N)) * float(N)
        fmax = max(1.0, 0.5 * float(N) * float(n) / float(m))
        F = F * np.exp(-(freqs / fmax) ** 2)
        y = np.real(ifft(F))
    return np.maximum(y, 0.0)


def add_noise(a, rng, scale):
    """Add small Gaussian noise and renormalize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    scale = float(scale)
    if scale <= 0.0:
        return normalize_seq(a, S)
    out = a + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
    return normalize_seq(out, S)


def to_final_list(a):
    """Convert candidate array to a clean Python list."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return [1.0]
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = normalize_seq(a, S)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def update_best(global_best, global_a, a_new, sc, label=""):
    """Return updated best pair if candidate improves it."""
    if a_new is None:
        return global_best, global_a
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return global_best, global_a
    if global_a is None or sc < global_best - 1e-12:
        try:
            print("  improved [%s] score=%.6f n=%d" % (label, float(sc), len(a_new)))
        except Exception:
            pass
        return float(sc), np.asarray(a_new, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    return global_best, global_a


def make_starts(base, n, rng, count_low=3):
    """Create diverse initial sequences of length n."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 1
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    raw = []

    if base is not None:
        b = resample_seq(base, n)
        raw.append(("base", b))

    if n == 1:
        t = np.array([0.0])
    else:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)

    raw.append(("uniform", np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (S / float(n))))
    raw.append(("cos_edge", 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)))
    raw.append(("cos_center", 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (t - 0.5))))
    raw.append(("linear", np.linspace(0.02, 1.0, n)))

    for aexp in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        v = (t ** aexp) * ((1.0 - t) ** aexp) + 1e-4
        raw.append(("beta_%g" % aexp, v))

    for center in (0.20, 0.30, 0.70, 0.80):
        v = np.exp(-(((t - center) / 0.10) ** 2))
        raw.append(("gauss_%g" % center, v))

    for i in range(int(count_low)):
        v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for fr in (1, 2, 3, 5):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.35)
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            v = v + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(fr) * t + phase)
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.01)
        raw.append(("randlow_%d" % i, v))

    raw.append(("rand", rng.random(n) + 0.1))

    res = []
    noise_base = 0.002 * S / np.sqrt(float(n))
    for name, v in raw:
        ar = normalize_seq(v, S)
        ar = normalize_seq(ar + rng.normal(0.0, noise_base, n), S)
        res.append((name, ar))
    return res


def multiplicative_update(a, g, eta, floor, S):
    """a <- normalize(a * exp(-eta * g/max(g))) with a small floor."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    gm = float(g.max())
    if gm < 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    fac = np.exp(-float(eta) * (g / gm))
    out = a * fac
    np.maximum(out, floor, out=out)
    return normalize_seq(out, S)


def run_log_descent(a_in, budget_s, rng,
                    beta_start=15.0,
                    beta_end=1500.0,
                    top_frac=0.008,
                    top_prob_start=0.25,
                    top_prob_end=0.65,
                    eta_values=(0.1, 0.5, 2.0)):
    """
    Multiplicative natural-gradient descent in log-amplitude space.
    The state is kept normalized to sum sqrt(2n).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(len(a))
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1), np.inf
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = normalize_seq(a, S)
    floor = S / (float(n) * 1e8)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total_budget

    current = score_fft(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1

        c, A, N = fft_selfconv(a, N)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 1e-300:
            a = np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
            current = score_fft(a)
        else:
            current = float(2.0 * n * c.max() / (s * s))

        if current < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = current
            best = a.copy()

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        tnorm = min(1.0, elapsed / total_budget if total_budget > 0.0 else 0.0)
        beta = beta_start + (beta_end - beta_start) * min(1.0, 0.9 * tnorm)
        use_top_prob = top_prob_start + (top_prob_end - top_prob_start) * tnorm

        if rng.random() < use_top_prob:
            w = top_weights(c, top_frac, min_count=3)
        else:
            w = lse_weights(c, beta)

        W = fft(w, N)
        # s_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}, valid linear cross-correlation.
        svec = np.real(ifft(A * np.conj(W)))[:n]
        svec = np.maximum(svec, 0.0)
        g = 2.0 * a * svec
        g = g / max(1e-12, current)

        gm = float(g.max())
        if gm < 1e-300:
            a = add_noise(a, rng, 0.02 * S / np.sqrt(float(n)))
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        best_trial = current
        best_cand = a
        for eta in eta_values:
            cand = multiplicative_update(a, g, float(eta), floor, S)
            sc, _, _ = fft_selfconv(cand, N)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs < 1e-300:
                sc = np.inf
            else:
                sc = float(2.0 * n * float(sc.max()) / (cs * cs))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial:
                best_trial = sc
                best_cand = cand

        if best_trial < current - 1e-11:
            a = best_cand
            current = best_trial
            no_improve = 0
            if current < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = current
                best = a.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 12:
                noise = 0.025 * S / np.sqrt(float(n)) * np.exp(-0.08 * float(no_improve))
                a = add_noise(a, rng, noise)
                no_improve = 0

    return best, best_score


def run_spectral_gs(a_in, budget_s, rng, m_spacing=0.004):
    """
    Spectral square-root GS.  Project current convolution into a bounded box,
    take a DFT square root with branch choices, and project back to nonnegative
    sequences.  Uses random sign flips to explore square-root branches.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(len(a))
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1), np.inf
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    S2 = S * S
    a = normalize_seq(a, S)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    avg = S2 / float(L)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    current = score_fft(a, N)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current
    M = max(avg * (1.0 + 1e-9), min(current * (1.0 - m_spacing), current))
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1
        c, A, N = fft_selfconv(a, N)
        cs = float(a.sum())
        if cs < 1e-300:
            a = np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
            current = score_fft(a, N)
        else:
            current = float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()) / (cs * cs))

        if current < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = current
            best = a.copy()

        if M >= current:
            M = max(avg * (1.0 + 1e-9), current * (1.0 - m_spacing))
        M = max(avg * (1.0 + 1e-9), min(M, current * (1.0 - 0.0025)))

        q = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        qs = float(q.sum())
        if qs > 1e-300:
            q = q * (S2 / qs)
        q_t = project_q_box(q, S2, M)
        Q = fft(q_t, N)

        best_trial = current
        best_cand = a

        # Two branches: local nearest branch and a randomly perturbed branch.
        for flip_prob in (0.0, 0.22):
            Pn = spectral_sqrt_nearest(Q, A, N, rng=rng, flip_prob=flip_prob)
            p = np.real(ifft(Pn))[:n]
            p = project_simplex(p, S)

            for alpha in (1.0, 0.5):
                cand = (1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * p
                cand = normalize_seq(cand, S)
                c2, _, _ = fft_selfconv(cand, N)
                cs2 = float(cand.sum())
                if cs2 < 1e-300:
                    sc = np.inf
                else:
                    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(c2.max()) / (cs2 * cs2))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial:
                    best_trial = sc
                    best_cand = cand

        if best_trial < current - 1e-11:
            a = best_cand
            current = best_trial
            stall = 0
            if current < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = current
                best = a.copy()
            M = max(avg * (1.0 + 1e-9), min(current * (1.0 - 0.004), M * 0.985))
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 25:
                M = min(max(avg * (1.0 + 1e-9), current * (1.0 + 0.02)), M * 1.15)
                stall = 0
        M = max(avg * (1.0 + 1e-9), M * 0.9992)

    return best, best_score


def run_low_res_search(base, n_low, budget_s, rng):
    """Quick multi-start search at low resolution for shape transfer."""
    n_low = int(max(128, min(2048, n_low)))
    starts = make_starts(base, n_low, rng, count_low=6)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    best = None
    best_score = np.inf
    nstarts = len(starts)
    per = max(1.0, 0.75 * (deadline - t0) / float(max(1, nstarts)))

    for name, a in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        a1, s1 = run_log_descent(a, per, rng)
        if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
            best_score = s1
            best = a1

    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if best is not None and remaining > 3.0:
        a2, s2 = run_spectral_gs(best, 0.6 * remaining, rng)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = s2
            best = a2

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2100, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Searches within the supplied wall-clock budget and
    returns the best non-negative sequence found.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + (budget_s - 40.0)

    # Load parent sequence if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
        parent_arr = np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        parent_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(parent_arr), parent_arr, 0.0)
        parent_arr = np.maximum(parent_arr, 0.0)
        if parent_arr.size >= 64 and float(parent_arr.sum()) > 0.0:
            parent = parent_arr
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Choose primary search length.
    if parent is not None and 2048 <= parent.size <= 16384:
        n_main = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n_main = 8192

    print("Starting: seed=%d budget=%.1fs main_n=%d parent=%s" %
          (int(seed), budget_s, n_main, ("yes len=%d" % parent.size) if parent is not None else "no"))

    global_best = np.inf
    global_a = None

    # Build and evaluate main initial candidates.
    main_starts = make_starts(parent, n_main, rng, count_low=3)
    scored = []
    for name, a in main_starts:
        sc = score_fft(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, name, a))
            global_best, global_a = update_best(global_best, global_a, a, sc, "init_" + name)

    # Low-resolution exploration for shape transfer.
    low_best = None
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    low_budget = min(90.0, 0.055 * max(0.0, remaining))
    if low_budget > 25.0:
        n_low = max(256, min(1024, max(256, n_main // 8)))
        low_best, _ = run_low_res_search(parent, n_low, low_budget, rng)
        if low_best is not None:
            low_up = resample_seq(low_best, n_main)
            S_main = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_main))
            low_up = normalize_seq(low_up + rng.normal(0.0, 0.002 * S_main / np.sqrt(n_main), n_main), S_main)
            sc_up = score_fft(low_up)
            if np.isfinite(sc_up):
                scored.append((sc_up, "low_res_upsampled", low_up))
                global_best, global_a = update_best(global_best, global_a, low_up, sc_up, "low_up")

    scored.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))

    # Order: low-res upsample first if present/competitive, then top initial starts.
    search_order = []
    for sc, name, arr in scored:
        if name == "low_res_upsampled" and arr is not None and all(x is not arr for x in search_order):
            if len(search_order) < 1:
                search_order.append(arr)
    for sc, name, arr in scored:
        if arr is None:
            continue
        if all(x is not arr for x in search_order):
            search_order.append(arr)
        if len(search_order) >= 7:
            break

    max_starts = 4 if n_main <= 8192 else 3
    caps_log = (520.0, 360.0, 300.0, 240.0)
    caps_spec = (210.0, 130.0, 110.0, 90.0)

    for idx in range(min(max_starts, len(search_order))):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 120.0:
            break
        start = search_order[idx]
        log_cap = caps_log[min(idx, len(caps_log) - 1)]
        spec_cap = caps_spec[min(idx, len(caps_spec) - 1)]
        log_b = min(0.5 * rem, log_cap)
        if log_b < 25.0:
            break

        a1, s1 = run_log_descent(start, log_b, rng)
        label = "main_%d" % idx
        global_best, global_a = update_best(global_best, global_a, a1, s1, label)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        spec_b = min(0.25 * rem, spec_cap)
        if global_a is not None and spec_b > 20.0:
            a2, s2 = run_spectral_gs(global_a, spec_b, rng)
            global_best, global_a = update_best(global_best, global_a, a2, s2, label + "_spec")

    # Optional length doubling / coarser alternative.
    best_main_a = global_a.copy() if global_a is not None and global_a.size == n_main else None
    best_main_score = global_best if global_a is not None and global_a.size == n_main else np.inf

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if global_a is not None and rem > 420.0 and global_a.size < 16384:
        alt_n = min(16384, int(global_a.size) * 2)
        if alt_n > global_a.size:
            print("Trying alternate length n=%d after %.0fs" % (alt_n, time.monotonic() - t0))
            S_alt = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(alt_n))
            alt_start = resample_seq(global_a, alt_n)
            alt_start = normalize_seq(
                alt_start + rng.normal(0.0, 0.002 * S_alt / np.sqrt(alt_n), alt_n),
                S_alt
            )
            log_b = min(0.45 * rem, 360.0)
            a1, s1 = run_log_descent(alt_start, log_b, rng)
            global_best, global_a = update_best(global_best, global_a, a1, s1, "alt_n")

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            spec_b = min(0.20 * rem, 120.0)
            if global_a is not None and spec_b > 20.0:
                a2, s2 = run_spectral_gs(global_a, spec_b, rng)
                global_best, global_a = update_best(global_best, global_a, a2, s2, "alt_n_spec")

    # Final polishing on current global best.
    polish_passes = 0
    while global_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 50.0 and polish_passes < 2:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 80.0:
            break
        Sg = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(len(global_a)))
        noise_scale = 0.0018 * Sg / np.sqrt(float(len(global_a))) * (0.6 ** polish_passes)
        start = add_noise(global_a, rng, noise_scale)
        log_b = min(0.55 * rem, 430.0)
        a1, s1 = run_log_descent(start, log_b, rng)
        global_best, global_a = update_best(global_best, global_a, a1, s1, "polish%d_log" % polish_passes)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        spec_b = min(0.25 * rem, 160.0)
        if global_a is not None and spec_b > 20.0:
            a2, s2 = run_spectral_gs(global_a, spec_b, rng)
            global_best, global_a = update_best(global_best, global_a, a2, s2, "polish%d_spec" % polish_passes)
        polish_passes += 1

    # Fallback if everything failed.
    if global_a is None:
        global_a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        global_a = normalize_seq(global_a, np.sqrt(2.0 * 256))

    # Build final candidates and validate against the provided evaluator when possible.
    candidates = []
    if global_a is not None:
        candidates.append(global_a)
    if best_main_a is not None and global_a is not None and best_main_a.size != global_a.size:
        candidates.append(best_main_a)
    if not candidates:
        cand = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        candidates.append(cand)

    final_list = None
    final_score = np.inf
    for cand in candidates:
        cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if cand.size < 8 or float(cand.sum()) <= 0.0:
            continue
        if cand.size > 20000:
            # Avoid possibly extremely slow direct convolution in the external evaluator.
            continue
        L = to_final_list(cand)
        ver = np.inf
        try:
            ver = evaluate_sequence(L)
        except Exception:
            ver = score_fft(np.asarray(L, dtype=np.float64))
        if np.isfinite(ver) and ver < final_score:
            final_score = ver
            final_list = L

    if final_list is None:
        # Safe fallback.
        fb = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        fb = normalize_seq(fb, np.sqrt(2.0 * 256))
        final_list = [float(x) for x in fb]
        final_score = np.inf
        try:
            final_score = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        except Exception:
            final_score = score_fft(np.asarray(final_list, dtype=np.float64))

    print("Final: returned n=%d internal_or_eval_score=%.6f elapsed=%.1fs" %
          (len(final_list), final_score, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return final_list
```
